Register namespace before parsing\n    ET.register_namespace('hp', NS_HP)\n\n    tree = ET.parse(xml_path)\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    replacements_made = []\n    modified = False\n\n    # Find all paragraph elements\n    for para in root.findall('.//hp:p', NSMAP):\n        # Get all runs in this paragraph\n        runs = para.findall('hp:run', NSMAP)\n        if not runs:\n            continue\n\n        # Reconstruct full text from all runs and text elements\n        full_text_parts = []\n        run_text_info = []  # Store (run, t_element, text) tuples\n\n        for run in runs:\n            t_elems = run.findall('hp:t', NSMAP)\n            for t_elem in t_elems:\n                text = t_elem.text or ''\n                full_text_parts.append(text)\n                run_text_info.append((run, t_elem, text))\n\n        full_text = ''.join(full_text_parts)\n\n        # Check if this paragraph contains placeholders\n        if has_placeholders(full_text):\n            # Replace placeholders\n            new_text = replace_placeholders(full_text, data)\n\n            # Record the replacement\n            placeholders = find_placeholders(full_text)\n            for ph in placeholders:\n                if ph in data:\n                    replacements_made.append(f\"{ph}: {data[ph]}\")\n\n            # Clear all existing runs' text elements\n            for run, t_elem, _ in run_text_info:\n                run.remove(t_elem)\n\n            # Remove empty runs\n            for run in list(runs):\n                if len(run) == 0:\n                    para.remove(run)\n\n            # Create a new consolidated run with the replaced text\n            # Keep attributes from the first run if available\n            first_run = runs[0] if runs else None\n            if first_run is not None and len(first_run) > 0:\n                # Use existing run, just add a new text element\n                new_t = ET.Element('{%s}t' % NS_HP)\n                new_t.text = new_text\n                first_run.append(new_t)\n            else:\n                # Create a new run\n                new_run = ET.Element('{%s}run' % NS_HP)\n                if first_run is not None:\n                    # Copy attributes from first run\n                    for attr, val in first_run.attrib.items():\n                        new_run.set(attr, val)\n                new_t = ET.Element('{%s}t' % NS_HP)\n                new_t.text = new_text\n                new_run.append(new_t)\n                para.append(new_run)\n\n            # Remove any extra runs that might be left\n            remaining_runs = para.findall('hp:run', NSMAP)\n            for run in remaining_runs[1:]:\n                para.remove(run)\n\n            # Remove lineSegArray elements from this paragraph\n            for linesegarray in para.findall('hp:lineSegArray', NSMAP):\n                para.remove(linesegarray)\n\n            modified = True\n\n    if modified:\n        # Write back with proper encoding and XML declaration\n        tree.write(xml_path, encoding='UTF-8', xml_declaration=True)\n\n    return replacements_made\n\n\ndef process_hwpx():\n    \"\"\"Main processing function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading JSON data...\")\n    data = load_json_data(JSON_PATH)\n    print(f\"Loaded data: {data}\")\n\n    # Create temporary directory for extraction\n    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:\n        print(f\"Extracting HWPX to {temp_dir}...\")\n\n        # Extract HWPX (ZIP) archive\n        with zipfile.ZipFile(TEMPLATE_PATH, 'r') as zf:\n            zf.extractall(temp_dir)\n\n        # Find and process all XML files in Contents directory\n        contents_dir = os.path.join(temp_dir, 'Contents')\n        all_replacements = []\n\n        if os.path.isdir(contents_dir):\n            for filename in os.listdir(contents_dir):\n                if filename.endswith('.xml'):\n                    xml_path = os.path.join(contents_dir, filename)\n                    print(f\"Processing {filename}...\")\n                    replacements = process_xml_file(xml_path, data)\n                    all_replacements.extend(replacements)\n\n        # Repackage the HWPX file\n        print(f\"Repackaging to {OUTPUT_PATH}...\")\n        with zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT_PATH, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:\n            # Walk through the temp directory and add all files\n            for root, dirs, files in os.walk(temp_dir):\n                for file in files:\n                    file_path = os.path.join(root, file)\n                    arcname = os.path.relpath(file_path, temp_dir)\n                    zf.write(file_path, arcname)\n\n    print(f\"Done! Output saved to {OUTPUT_PATH}\")\n    return all_replacements\n\n\ndef validate_output():\n    \"\"\"Validate the output file has no remaining placeholders.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Validation ===\")\n\n    # Check it's a valid ZIP\n    try:\n        with zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT_PATH, 'r') as zf:\n            print(f\"✓ Valid ZIP archive\")\n            namelist = zf.namelist()\n            print(f\"  Contains {len(namelist)} files\")\n\n            # Check all XML files for remaining placeholders\n            remaining = []\n            for name in namelist:\n                if name.endswith('.xml'):\n                    content = zf.read(name).decode('utf-8')\n                    if '{{' in content or '}}' in content:\n                        remaining.append(name)\n                        # Show the context\n                        for match in re.finditer(r'\\{\\{.*?\\}\\}', content):\n                            print(f\"  ! Reconstruct full text from all runs and text elements\n        full_text_parts = []\n        run_text_info = []  # Store (run, t_element, text) tuples\n\n        for run in runs:\n            t_elems = run.findall('hp:t', NSMAP)\n            for t_elem in t_elems:\n                text = t_elem.text or ''\n                full_text_parts.append(text)\n                run_text_info.append((run, t_elem, text))\n\n        full_text = ''.join(full_text_parts)\n\n        # Record the replacement\n            placeholders = find_placeholders(full_text)\n            for ph in placeholders:\n                if ph in data:\n                    replacements_made.append(f\"{ph}: {data[ph]}\")\n\n            # Get the first run to preserve its attributes\n            first_run = runs[0]\n            first_run_char_pr = first_run.get('charPrIDRef')\n\n            # Remove all existing runs\n            for run in list(runs):\n                para.remove(run)\n\n            # Create a new consolidated run with the replaced text\n            new_run = etree.Element('{%s}run' % NS_HP)\n            if first_run_char_pr:\n                new_run.set('charPrIDRef', first_run_char_pr)\n\n            new_t = etree.Element('{%s}t' % NS_HP)\n            new_t.text = new_text\n            new_run.append(new_t)\n\n            # Append the new run\n            para.append(new_run)\n\n            # Remove lineSegArray elements from this paragraph\n            for linesegarray in para.findall('hp:lineSegArray', NSMAP):\n                para.remove(linesegarray)\n\n            modified = True\n\n    if modified:\n        # Write back with proper encoding and XML declaration\n        tree.write(xml_path, encoding='UTF-8', xml_declaration=True)\n\n    return replacements_made\n\n\ndef process_hwpx():\n    \"\"\"Main processing function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading JSON data...\")\n    data = load_json_data(JSON_PATH)\n    print(f\"Loaded data: {data}\")\n\n    # Find and process all XML files in Contents directory\n        contents_dir = os.path.join(temp_dir, 'Contents')\n        all_replacements = []\n\n        if os.path.isdir(contents_dir):\n            for filename in os.listdir(contents_dir):\n                if filename.endswith('.xml'):\n                    xml_path = os.path.join(contents_dir, filename)\n                    print(f\"Processing {filename}...\")\n                    replacements = process_xml_file(xml_path, data)\n                    all_replacements.extend(replacements)\n\n        # Repackage the HWPX file\n        print(f\"Repackaging to {OUTPUT_PATH}...\")\n        with zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT_PATH, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:\n            # Walk through the temp directory and add all files\n            for root, dirs, files in os.walk(temp_dir):\n                for file in files:\n                    file_path = os.path.join(root, file)\n                    arcname = os.path.relpath(file_path, temp_dir)\n                    zf.write(file_path, arcname)\n\n    print(f\"Done! Register namespace before parsing\n    ET.register_namespace('hp', NS_HP)\n\n    tree = ET.parse(xml_path)\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    replacements_made = []\n    modified = False\n\n    # Find all paragraph elements\n    for para in root.findall('.//hp:p', NSMAP):\n        # Get all runs in this paragraph\n        runs = para.findall('hp:run', NSMAP)\n        if not runs:\n            continue\n\n        # Reconstruct full text from all runs and text elements\n        full_text_parts = []\n        run_text_info = []  # Store (run, t_element, text) tuples\n\n        for run in runs:\n            t_elems = run.findall('hp:t', NSMAP)\n            for t_elem in t_elems:\n                text = t_elem.text or ''\n                full_text_parts.append(text)\n                run_text_info.append((run, t_elem, text))\n\n        full_text = ''.join(full_text_parts)\n\n        # Check if this paragraph contains placeholders\n        if has_placeholders(full_text):\n            # Replace placeholders\n            new_text = replace_placeholders(full_text, data)\n\n            # Record the replacement\n            placeholders = find_placeholders(full_text)\n            for ph in placeholders:\n                if ph in data:\n                    replacements_made.append(f\"{ph}: {data[ph]}\")\n\n            # Clear all existing runs' text elements\n            for run, t_elem, _ in run_text_info:\n                run.remove(t_elem)\n\n            # Remove empty runs\n            for run in list(runs):\n                if len(run) == 0:\n                    para.remove(run)\n\n            # Create a new consolidated run with the replaced text\n            # Keep attributes from the first run if available\n            first_run = runs[0] if runs else None\n            if first_run is not None and len(first_run) > 0:\n                # Use existing run, just add a new text element\n                new_t = ET.Element('{%s}t' % NS_HP)\n                new_t.text = new_text\n                first_run.append(new_t)\n            else:\n                # Create a new run\n                new_run = ET.Element('{%s}run' % NS_HP)\n                if first_run is not None:\n                    # Copy attributes from first run\n                    for attr, val in first_run.attrib.items():\n                        new_run.set(attr, val)\n                new_t = ET.Element('{%s}t' % NS_HP)\n                new_t.text = new_text\n                new_run.append(new_t)\n                para.append(new_run)\n\n            # Remove any extra runs that might be left\n            remaining_runs = para.findall('hp:run', NSMAP)\n            for run in remaining_runs[1:]:\n                para.remove(run)\n\n            # Remove lineSegArray elements from this paragraph\n            for linesegarray in para.findall('hp:lineSegArray', NSMAP):\n                para.remove(linesegarray)\n\n            modified = True\n\n    if modified:\n        #
